# RMIS Project Worklog

---
Task ID: 1
Agent: Z.ai Code (main agent)
Task: Adopt the uploaded `rmis-deploy-2026-10-12.tar.gz` as our own project, set it up, and run it.

Work Log:
- Extracted `upload/rmis-deploy-2026-10-12.tar.gz` to /tmp/rmis-extract and inspected contents: a complete Next.js 16 (App Router) RMIS — Recruitment Management Information System for DOST-MIRDC, with Prisma/SQLite schema, seed script, API routes, hash-router SPA frontend, and deployment docs.
- Reviewed `package.json` (same base scaffold name `nextjs_tailwind_shadcn_ts`, Next 16.1.1, Prisma 6), `.env.example`, `prisma/seed.ts`, `DEPLOYMENT.md` (two-tier access runbook), and `Caddyfile` (identical to sandbox gateway config).
- Replaced the scaffold in /home/z/my-project with the archive contents (kept `upload/` and `examples/`).
- Wrote `.env`: DATABASE_URL=file:/home/z/my-project/db/custom.db (sandbox shell env var already pins this path), generated random NEXTAUTH_SECRET, NEXTAUTH_URL=http://localhost:3000, INTRANET_ENFORCEMENT=off (so ADMIN/EVALUATOR logins work from any IP in the sandbox), EMAIL_PROVIDER=mock.
- Reinstalled dependencies: `rm -rf node_modules bun.lock .next && bun install` (882 packages) + `bun pm trust --all`.
- Pushed schema and generated client: `bun run db:push` (SQLite in sync, Prisma Client v6.19.3 generated).
- Seeded database: `bun prisma/seed.ts` → accounts testadmin / testevaluator / testapplicant / maria.santos (all password123), eligibility refs, courses, places of assignment, 4 job postings, sample applicants + applications.
- Started dev server in background (`bun run dev`, port 3000). `/api/health` → {"status":"healthy","checks":{"app":"ok","database":"ok"}}.
- Browser verification (agent-browser): public landing renders Job Opportunities board with seeded postings; admin login (testadmin) → Operations dashboard with live stats (Applicants 2, Active jobs 3, Applications 2, Shortlisted 1); Recruitment workspace lists 4 postings with Open/Closed statuses; applicant login (testapplicant) → applicant home with profile stats (14% profile progress, Apply Now board); onboarding tour modal dismisses; logout works.
- Mobile check (390×844): job cards stack correctly, bottom tab bar + footer behave properly.
- Fixed ESLint errors from `deploy/mirdc_middleware/index.js` (CommonJS require in the separate office email-relay service) by adding `deploy/**` and `scripts/**` to eslint ignores. `bun run lint` now passes clean.
- No console errors, no runtime errors in dev.log.

Stage Summary:
- RMIS is now our own project at /home/z/my-project and runs on port 3000.
- Demo logins (password123): testadmin (ADMIN), testevaluator (EVALUATOR), testapplicant (APPLICANT), maria.santos.
- Key config: INTRANET_ENFORCEMENT=off for sandbox demo; EMAIL_PROVIDER=mock (emails only recorded to ledger); Turnstile captcha auto-disabled (no keys configured) on trusted gates.
- App is a single-route hash-router SPA (#/operations, #/recruitment, #/home, #/vacant-positions, #/signin …).

Task ID: 2
Agent: Z.ai Code (main agent)
Task: Mobile UX fixes — (a) decrease footer height on mobile, (b) remove container-in-container nesting in profile forms on mobile, (c) fix crushed table columns on mobile using the authentic BEUI Table (no recreation).

Work Log:
- Verified table authenticity: fetched https://beui.dev/r/table.json and diffed all 12 vendored files in src/components/motion/table — every file is BYTE-FOR-BYTE identical to the official BEUI registry (index.tsx differs by one provenance comment only). The block is authentic; no recreation needed.
- Diagnosed the mobile table crush: the BEUI block floors the table at its min-column width and lets the viewport scroll, but flexible (undeclared) columns split the leftover with the block's trailing filler col under table-layout:fixed — Position/Candidate columns collapsed to the 64px floor on phones.
- Fixed at the CALL SITE (sanctioned BEUI usage): responsive column sets below md via useIsMobile() —
  • recruitment.tsx: mobile [Position 142px (logo hidden, deadline folded into subtitle), Status 108px, Actions 108px]; sm+ keeps the full 7-column table with editable vacancies.
  • review-queue.tsx: mobile [Candidate 106px (position folded into subtitle, avatar hidden), Status 134px, Actions 118px (single Review/View button)].
  • candidates.tsx: mobile [Candidate 166px (avatar hidden), Profile 120px, quick-view Actions 72px]; application count stays in quick view.
  • settings.tsx UsersPanel: mobile [User 208px (role folded into subtitle), combined Actions 150px carrying the Active switch + Edit + Delete]; sm+ keeps the 4-column table with 480px User column.
  • Added max-sm:[&_td]:px-3 max-sm:[&_th]:px-3 to those Table classNames (block cells wear px-4; 8px per cell back on phones). recruitment.tsx needed the missing `cn` import (caught at runtime, fixed).
- Footer (shell/footer.tsx): mobile strip tightened further — py-2, gap-0.5, 10px note with snug leading, size-6 chip, h-12 seal watermark below sm. Measured footer height on 390px viewport: ~97px → 77px. sm+ (desktop) sizing untouched.
- Profile forms (shell.tsx SectionCard + all 7 profile sections): added opt-in `bareOnMobile` prop — below sm the outer section card drops its chrome (no border/surface/radius/inset), the title wraps and the header actions drop to their own full-width row, so the disclosure groups ("Identity", "Address", …) and entry cards become the ONLY containers. Applied to personal-info, education, work-experience, training, eligibility, awards, documents sections. Desktop (sm+) keeps the card anatomy.
- Browser verification at 390×844: recruitment/review-queue/candidates/settings tables all render fully readable without crushed columns; profile Section 01 shows one container level; footer is a tight 77px strip. Desktop regression at 1280×800: full 7-column recruitment table intact, profile card-in-card anatomy preserved, footer at 80px (Task 37 sizing unchanged). bun run lint clean; /api/health healthy.

Stage Summary:
- Mobile footer height reduced ~20% (97px → 77px on 390px viewport).
- Profile forms on mobile are flat: section header as plain heading, inner group cards as the only containers; desktop unchanged.
- Tables on mobile use responsive column sets at the call sites; the BEUI Table block remains 100% official vendored code (registry-verified), untouched.

Task ID: 3
Agent: Z.ai Code (main agent)
Task: Profile page → read-only preview by default; "Save Changes" becomes "Edit Profile"; editing is an explicit session (user request, design left to me).

Work Log:
- Reworked src/components/views/profile/personal-info-section.tsx (Task 40):
  • PREVIEW BY DEFAULT: the section renders the SAVED record as quiet label/value rows inside the same four disclosure groups (Identity / Address / Legal Disclosures / Character References) — real read-only text, never disabled inputs. Empty values show "—" + sr-only "Not provided"; dates format human-readable (formatDate → "May 12, 1990"); Yes/No declarations render as Yes/No; character references render as name-led cards listing only filled fields, with a quiet empty note when none exist.
  • "Edit Profile" button (pencil, dlg-cta) replaces "Save Changes" in the header; description swaps to "Read-only preview — click Edit Profile to make changes." / "Editing — your changes apply when you save."
  • EDIT SESSION: enterEdit seeds the draft from the freshest server profile; header becomes SaveHint ("Unsaved changes") + Cancel + Save Changes (official StatefulButton lifecycle). Save validates through the Task 32 auth timing (touchAll + hasGaps), persists, toasts, refreshes session, fires the new onChanged parent reload BEFORE leaving edit mode so the preview shows server truth (no stale-value flash), then flashes "Saved ✓" in the preview header (useSavedFlash).
  • AUTOSAVE RETIRED: with a Cancel affordance, silent persistence would make "discard" a lie — edits only mark the draft dirty; the 1.2s debounce + timer plumbing is gone.
  • CANCEL: instant exit when the draft is clean; AlertDialog "Discard unsaved changes?" (Keep editing / Discard changes, ghostBadBtn) when dirty or in error.
  • NEW-RECORD BOOT: an empty record (no first+last name+email) mounts straight into edit mode so first-time applicants still land on the form; filled records get the preview.
  • Reports unsaved state upward via onUnsavedChange (false on unmount), takes onChanged for the post-save silent reload.
- Reworked src/components/views/profile-view.tsx: Task 40 unsaved-changes navigation guard — hasUnsaved state + stable handleUnsavedChange; handleMobileSectionChange renamed changeSection; new requestSection()/requestPortalExit() wrap EVERY nav path (desktop step band, mobile strip cells, caption prev/next, footer Back/Continue, Return to Portal); while dirty, navigation opens "Leave with unsaved changes?" and confirmLeave() resumes the ORIGINAL destination (pendingNav holds section number or "home"). PersonalInfoSection now receives onChanged={reloadSilent} + onUnsavedChange. Also dropped unused EmptyState/ghostBtn imports (lint hygiene).
- Browser verification (agent-browser, testapplicant @ #/profile):
  • Preview: zero textboxes in the a11y tree, Edit Profile present, values/dashes render correctly (desktop 1280×800).
  • Edit: form returns with Cancel/Save Changes; dirty → "Unsaved changes"; Cancel w/ dirty → discard dialog; Keep editing stays; Discard returns to preview with value unchanged (verified no leak).
  • Save: middle name "Reyes" → toast "Profile changes saved", header "Saved ✓" flash, preview shows the saved value, Identity chip 4/13→5/13; then reverted to original data via the same flow.
  • Validation: clearing First Name + Save reveals "First Name is required." and stays in edit (Task 32 timing intact).
  • Nav guard: dirty + step-band click → "Leave with unsaved changes?"; Keep editing stays (still editing+dirty); Discard & leave lands on Education; returning to section 01 shows a fresh preview (no session leak).
  • Mobile 390×844: preview = full-width Edit Profile under the title, single-column rows, no card nesting (Task 39 intact); edit = Cancel/Save row; discard dialog renders cleanly.
  • Tooling note: mid-session agent-browser clicks silently stopped reaching the page (0 pointer events at document level even via raw mouse down/up) — a stale daemon issue, NOT an app bug; `agent-browser close` + fresh sign-in restored real clicks (sign-in button click worked). JS-dispatched clicks were used to complete verification after the restart.
- bun run lint clean; dev.log shows no runtime errors; test data restored to its pre-test state (Middle Name back to empty).

Stage Summary:
- Profile page is now a READ-ONLY PREVIEW of the saved record; editing requires the explicit "Edit Profile" → form → "Save Changes" (or Cancel/discard) session.
- Autosave on section 01 is retired by design; data-loss paths are guarded (Cancel confirm + view-level navigation guard covering step band, mobile strip, caption arrows, footer Back/Continue, Return to Portal).
- New records skip the preview and boot into the form; successful saves return to the preview with server-truth data and a transient "Saved ✓" flash.
- Sections 02–07 untouched (they were already list-based: read-only entries + explicit Add/Edit per entry).

Section switch away & back → boots PREVIEW (mode reset verified).

Section 01 keeps its draft session (Save Changes/Cancel + discard confirm + navigation guard); sections 02–07 use the immediate-persistence edit gate (Edit Profile ↔ Done Editing, empty sections boot in edit mode).

Task ID: 6
Agent: Z.ai Code (main)
Task: Implement the 2026-10-13 MOM changes: MOM rejection/shortlist email templates, recruitment-mailer identity, required profile fields (email/phone/character refs), rejection auto-email, re-application for previously rejected applicants, invitation dialog rework (Invitation Details + face-to-face/online mode), and bulk invitations to shortlisted applicants.

Work Log:
- Templates (src/lib/notify.ts): replaced emailRegretLetter + emailShortlisted with the EXACT MOM wording — subjects "Application Status Update – {job}" / "Application Update – Shortlisted for {job}", greeting uses the applicant's LAST name, sign-off "MIRDC HR Office". Interview/exam invitation templates rebuilt around NoticeDetails: Date/Time/{Kind} Mode: Face-to-Face|Online/Venua or Meeting Link lines (mirrors the real MIRDC notice format), long-text Invitation Details block, attendance-confirmation line. Added RECRUITMENT_EMAIL_ADDRESS (EMAIL_FROM, default recruitment@mirdc.dost.gov.ph) + RECRUITMENT_SENDER_NAME ("MIRDC HR Office") and the `from` header on the middleware/resend drivers (MOM: nodemailer on the backend using the recruitment email); .env gains EMAIL_FROM.
- Subject-prefix plumbing: regrets route idempotency now matches "Application Status Update –" (+ legacy "Application regret:"/"Application regret —"); notice-route history maps the new subject to type "regret".
- Validation: noticeSchema — notes (Invitation Details) raised to 4,000 chars, new mode enum + meetingLink; new bulkNoticeSchema.
- Schema: Application @@unique([applicantId, jobId]) → @@index (re-application); db:push clean; seed.ts ensureApplication switched to findFirst(active→latest).
- Apply gate (api/jobs/apply): duplicate 409 now only for ACTIVE applications (isActiveApplicationStatus in lib/status.ts; REJECTED/DECLINED/WITHDRAWN free the posting). Jobs wire unchanged; clients compute active state.
- Prior-rejection visibility: queue route computes per-row priorRejections {count,lastDate,lastReason} in memory (self-row excluded, withdrawals ignored); applications/[id] GET adds priorRejectionsFor.
- New route POST /api/evaluator/applications/invitations: bulk interview/exam notices to shortlisted-stage applications — shared payload (date/time/mode/venue|meetingLink/contact/Invitation Details/examType), mode guards (online⇒meetingLink required; f2f⇒venue required), per-type subject-prefix idempotency, fans out email+SMS+notification+audit.
- Profile: personal-info-section — Mobile Number now required (rule + required marker + helper), Email Address gains isEmailShape rule, CollapsibleGroup gained `required` prop (red asterisk + sr-only), Character References group marked required in edit+preview, empty-note mentions the requirement. profile-view — requestSection blocks forward moves past section 01 while the SAVED profile has 0 named character references (toast explains), completion caption updated. lib/profile-completion + lib/profile-completion(client) — personal requirement now includes mobile number + ≥1 character reference (apply gate + Mark Complete + ring).
- Contact: contact-view + account-settings channels updated to the MOM details — "MIRDC Compound, Gen. Santos Ave. Bicutan 1631, City of Taguig, Metro Manila 1631, Philippines", "8837-0431 loc. 440/441", "recruitment@mirdc.dost.gov.ph".
- UI (review-workspace): NoticeFormDialog exported + reworked — Setup mode radio toggle (Face-to-Face/Online) swapping Venue⇄Online meeting link (mode-aware client+server validation), "Notes"→"Invitation Details" long text (no maxLength), bulk mode (bulkIds>1 → POSTs bulk endpoint with summary toast). Prior-rejection amber banner (History icon, count, last applied date, last recorded reason) under the dossier header.
- UI (review-queue): QueueRow.priorRejections; amber "Previously rejected ×N" chip in the Candidate cell (desktop + mobile column sets) and as a kanban tag; "Send interview invitations (N)" action (shortlisted cohort) opening the shared dialog in bulk mode.
- Jobs re-apply (jobs-view + applicant-home): applied-state computed via isActiveApplicationStatus (JobCard/SummaryCard/hero/JobListCard prop/cancel appliedId); SummaryCard shows a warning alert "Previous application not shortlisted — you may re-apply…" + the submit button reads "Re-apply" when a prior terminal attempt exists; Cancel only for the active application.
- Data note: Maria gained a "Career Service Sub-Professional (First Level)" eligibility (id 2) so her E2E apply to Administrative Assistant I passes the live MQR gate (used by the verification below; also useful demo data).
- Verification (browser, admin + maria.santos + testapplicant): contact page shows the 3 MOM details (old phone/email gone). Profile: Edit→clear Mobile→Save ⇒ "Mobile Number is required." and save blocked; Character References * marker; forward nav 01→02 blocked with toast at 0 refs, lifts after saving ref (Maria flow via testapplicant). Rejection: admin marked Maria's app #3 Not Qualified ⇒ status Rejected + email_log row subject "Application Status Update – Administrative Assistant I" body "Dear Santos," + exact MOM paragraphs (mock provider). Re-apply: job-3 detail shows warning alert + Re-apply button (no Cancel), re-apply created app #4 (same job, Applied) — non-unique pair works. Evaluator: queue row #4 carries "Previously rejected ×1" (desktop + mobile 390×844), workspace banner shows count/date/last reason; shortlisting #4 emitted "Application Update – Shortlisted for Administrative Assistant I" with MOM body. Bulk invitations: "Send interview invitations (2)" dialog (2 shortlisted) with Online mode + meeting link + 242-char Invitation Details ⇒ 2 emails each containing "Interview Mode: Online", "Meeting Link: https://meet.google.com/mirdc-interview-2026", details block; API re-send ⇒ {sent:0, alreadySent:2} (idempotent); single workspace notice (F2F default) emits "Interview Mode: Face-to-Face" + Venue; notice-history rail + GET map both subjects correctly. bun run lint clean; tsc: no new errors (pre-existing pds/dialog/skills debt untouched); dev.log: no runtime errors, health {app: ok, database: ok}.

Task ID: 7
Agent: Z.ai Code (main)
Task: User QA round — (1) header must show the COMPLETE RMIS name, not just "RMIS"; (2) fix the intermittent double-click logout bug; (3) enterprise-look upgrade for the sign-in and registration pages (consistent across the system).

Work Log:
- Logout fix (src/components/shell/nav-rail.tsx — performSignOut, the single shared helper used by the nav-rail dropdown, mobile tab bar, ⌘K command menu and Account Settings): the old fire-and-forget fetch + immediate window.location.reload() let the reload abort the in-flight POST /api/auth/logout, so the session cookie survived and the user had to sign out twice. Now AWAITS the POST (raced against a 4s guard so an unreachable server can never trap the user) before navigate("signin") + reload, and the request rides keepalive:true so it survives a fast unload regardless.
- Complete name lockups: new shared const RMIS_FULL_NAME ("Recruitment Management Information System") in src/components/auth-enterprise.tsx; applied to the public site-header (two-line wordmark, sm+, same pattern as the signed-in rail/mobile bar), the AuthBrandPanel lockup, the reset-password card brand row, the desktop footer eyebrow (mobile keeps the compact © RMIS strip per Tasks 36–38) and the layout metadata title ("RMIS · Recruitment Management Information System").
- Enterprise auth family (new src/components/auth-enterprise.tsx): AuthMobileLockup (seal + RMIS + full name, lg:hidden — auth pages previously had NO brand on phones) and AuthSecureStrip (government-portal trust strip; "session" = authorized-use/audit notice, "privacy" = RA 10173 line). Sign-in card: white fill + lift shadow (dlg-card-plain anatomy; .dark .bg-white sweep keeps night mode), gov.ph letterhead eyebrow ("Republic of the Philippines · DOST-MIRDC") over a hairline rule heading the form, trust strip under the card. Sign-up: same treatment through the official SignUpForm's sanctioned className prop (bg-white + shadow; no vendored edits), letterhead header on the OTP card, RA 10173 notice + privacy strip below, wrapper aligned to max-w-sm. Reset-password joins the family (lockup below lg, brand row lg+, lift-shadow card, session strip). Duplicate-masthead fix: AuthMobileLockup carries only seal+name (letterhead lives once, inside each card); reset card brand row is lg+-only.
- Verification (agent-browser): desktop 1280×800 sign-in (header + panel + card + strip + footer all show the full name), sign-up (card + RA notice + privacy strip), reset (card + strip), landing header; mobile 390×844 sign-in and sign-up (lockup renders, no duplication, no overflow); dark-mode spot check (card flips to raised dark surface, strip correct). Logout golden path TWICE (testadmin, then testevaluator): single click → LOGOUT audit row in dev.log, POST /api/auth/logout 200, next /api/admin/stats 401, reload lands on #/signin showing the form and STAYS signed out. bun run lint clean; dev.log compiles clean, no runtime errors; console: no errors/warnings.
- Note: agent-browser real-pointer clicks went stale mid-session again (known issue) — JS-dispatched clicks (el.click()) used for menu interactions; all states verified via DOM text + dev.log.

Stage Summary:
- Sign-out is now deterministic (one click, every surface) because the reload can no longer race the server logout; the bug's root cause (unload aborting the cookie-clearing POST) is fixed at the shared helper.
- Every brand surface in the system now reads the complete name: public header, auth panel, rail (existing), mobile top bar (existing), reset card, footer (desktop), tab title.
- Sign-in / sign-up / reset-password share one enterprise anatomy — institutional lockup (mobile), letterhead-headed white lift-shadow cards, RA-trust strips — while all official BEUI blocks, handlers, validation and copy stayed untouched.

Task ID: 8
Agent: Z.ai Code (main)
Task: User request — replace the section 07 (Supporting Documents) upload UI with the FolderUpload component per the user's documented usage (<FolderUpload adapter={demoAdapter} name="…" />), while keeping the table below that displays the applicant's uploaded documents.

Work Log:
- Sourced the block: the live BEUI registry (beui.dev/r/registry.json, 143 items) has NO folder-upload item (404 on /r/folder-upload.json; sitemap has only project-folder/card-folder) — so the component was BUILT to the user's exact API contract in the house BEUI design language (EASE_OUT motion, token surfaces, reduced-motion aware).
- New src/components/folder-upload.tsx — FolderUpload: a folder card workspace (tab row = open-folder glyph + `name` + live "N files · total size" ledger + Clear list when settled) wrapping a dashed dropzone ("Drop files into {name}" / Browse) and an animated per-file ledger with REAL progress (only what the adapter reports — no invented progress), success/error status-icon swap, per-row retry and remove (removing an in-flight row ABORTS its signal). Transport lives entirely in the adapter: FolderUploadAdapter { upload(file, {onProgress, signal}), validate? }. Sequential uploads (SQLite-safe, mirrors the old batch contract); onAllSettled(okCount cumulative) after each batch/retry sweep; React 19 ref-as-prop exposing retryFailed() (host re-runs failed rows after fixing an external blocker). Dropzone stacks on mobile (full-width Browse pill below the copy) — no overflow at 390px.
- New src/examples/_demo.ts — demoAdapter (simulated ~1.1s eased-progress upload) so the user's snippet works verbatim.
- Rewired src/components/views/profile/documents-section.tsx: the old BEUI FileUpload rail + queue machinery replaced by <FolderUpload ref adapter name="Supporting Documents" />. The section owns the REAL adapter: uploadWithProgress XHR POST to /api/applicant/documents (true upload progress, same FormData file+category contract, { error } envelope + 401/403/500 fallbacks parsed identically to apiFetch). Category gate lives in the adapter: no category ⇒ every file's row fails with "Select a category before uploading" + the Task 31-b shake contract fires; picking a category AUTO-RETRIES the blocked batch (ref.synced before the sweep). Task ID: 10
Agent: Z.ai Code (main agent)
Task: User QA (screenshot) — the Applied/Under Review status badges render a paper-plane ("send message") glyph, identically on both stages, across the Review Queue filter chips and Analytics. Directive: study and standardize status badges/icons across the ENTIRE system (evaluator + admin pages, tables, navs, filters).

Work Log:
- Root cause audit: the paper plane came from the "info" variant glyph — STATUS_DOT_GLYPH.info = Send (shell.tsx), the CSS mask .status-pill.status-info (send SVG, globals.css), and the journey modal's Apply step (rail-journey-info.tsx). Applied and Under Review deliberately share the blue info TINT (TONE_TO_VARIANT.primary→info, STAGE_VARIANT), so both stages drew the identical plane everywhere a variant-driven glyph rendered. AnimatedBadge (StatusPill) was already info=(i) — dots vs pills disagreed too.
- Canonical vocabulary (single source in lib/status-ui.ts): STAGE_GLYPH = Applied→Inbox (received), Under Review→ScanSearch (being screened, mirrors the Start Review CTA), Shortlisted→Check, Rejected→X. PILL_STAGE_GLYPH record (+ glyphForStatus helper) re-points the two blue status FAMILIES on badges (applied/pending/submitted → Inbox; under review/for evaluation/screening/evaluation/final review/evaluated/in review → ScanSearch). StatusDot gained a glyph prop; StageDot, analytics funnel + donut legend, candidates mini-pipeline, candidate-detail rows and job-workspace pipeline headers all pass stage glyphs; StatusPill overlays the stage glyph on AnimatedBadge (static record lookup — react-hooks/static-components rejects JSX types from calls; pulse badges keep their spinner).
- CSS: .status-pill.status-info mask send→(i) circle; comments updated. Journey modal: Apply step Send→Inbox. Send now means only real send actions (email composer, regret letters, SMS test, submit buttons).
- Browser-verified 9 surfaces: queue filter chips (user's screenshot), queue kanban columns, queue table status pills, analytics funnel + status-distribution legend, candidates kanban, candidate-detail application rows + header pills, job-workspace pipeline, admin dashboard recent-activity pills, applicant journey modal. Applied/Under Review now visually distinct on every surface; hue language unchanged.
- bun run lint clean (fixed one static-components error along the way); dev.log clean; committed 08acd96.

Stage Summary:
- One stage → one glyph, system-wide: Applied=Inbox · Under Review=ScanSearch · Shortlisted=Check · Rejected=X, on every dot, pill, filter chip, funnel and pipeline across evaluator, admin and applicant surfaces; the info variant speaks (i) and the paper plane is reserved for genuine send actions.

Task ID: 10-e
Agent: Z.ai Code (main agent)
Task: Jobs table statuses don't match the system's statuses and badges — unify.

Work Log:
- Diagnosis: the staff Jobs table flattened job status to binary Open (green ✓) / Closed (gray ○) via JobStatusPill, while every other surface (vacant-positions board, public landing, job cards) speaks the deadline-aware ladder — the board even showed Closed as a RED raw CSS pill. Same job, two status stories.
- Canonical resolver: jobStatusView() in lib/job-meta.ts — labels from deadlineState (the system vocabulary), variants from the badge tints: Closed (unpublished or deadline passed) → bad/red ✕; Closing today / Closing in N days (≤7d) → warn/amber ⚠; N days left (≤30d) → neutral/gray ○; Open (>30d) → ok/green ✓; Open until filled → neutral. Returns family ("open"/"closed") which backs the table's existing Open/Closed status filter (amber closing-soon rows stay in the Open family).
- One pill everywhere: recruitment's JobStatusPill now renders jobStatusView through the official StatusPill/AnimatedBadge; jobs-view's hand-rolled CSS DeadlinePill deleted — the board cards + QuickView hero import the same JobStatusPill; the job workspace header inherits automatically. Dead variantForJobStatus removed from status-ui.
- Column fix: the widest label ("Closing in 3 days") truncated in the old 130px/108px status columns — desktop widened to 150px, mobile rebalanced 120/140/98 (same 358px budget) so badges render unclipped on both.
- Browser-verified end-to-end: created QA postings to exercise amber (deadline +3d) and green (>30d) states; staff table shows ✓Open / ⚠Closing in 3 days / ✕Closed / ○N days left; vacant-positions board renders IDENTICAL badges (board's red Closed upgraded from CSS pill to the official badge); workspace header inherits the ladder; filter Open → 5 rows incl. the amber one, Closed → only the overdue row; mobile 390×844 badges fit fully. QA postings deleted after.
- Screenshots: .verify/10e-jobs-table-ladder.png, 10e-vacant-board-badges.png, 10e-filter-open-family.png, 10e-workspace-header-badge.png, 10e-mobile-table-badges.png.
- bun run lint clean; dev.log clean.

Stage Summary:

Task ID: 18
Agent: Z.ai Code (main agent)
Task: Fix the "page is constantly reloading" bug reported by the user.

Work Log:
- Diagnosed via dev.log: ~82 log lines per 10s cycling GET / -> /api/jobs?limit=200 -> /api/session with interleaved "✓ Compiled" lines (617 GET / since the last restart); reproduced in a fresh agent-browser tab at ~47 full page loads per 12s; browser console showed "[Fast Refresh] rebuilding → done" cycling with "[HMR] connected" — a dev-watcher feedback loop, not an app bug (zero page errors).
- Isolated the trigger: appending ONE line to /home/z/my-project/dev.log fired 6 recompiles within 3s. Root cause: package.json's dev script was "next dev -p 3000 2>&1 | tee dev.log" — teeing the live log into the project root, which Turbopack watches. Every request appended dev.log → rebuild → Fast Refresh FULL reload → more requests → infinite loop. (Latent since Task 1; surfaced after today's repeated dev-server restarts.)
- Fix: dev script → "next dev -p 3000 > /tmp/rmis-dev.log 2>&1" (log lives OUTSIDE the watched tree); stale dev.log removed. NOTE FOR FUTURE SESSIONS: the live dev-server log is now /tmp/rmis-dev.log, NOT /home/z/my-project/dev.log.
- Verified: fresh visit → 0 requests and 0 rebuilds over 10s idle (was 166 lines / 47 reloads in 12s); page renders normally; no console errors; dev.log is not recreated on restart; production "start" script untouched (its tee writes server.log at runtime on the VM, where no watcher runs).

Stage Summary:
- Commit 4e327b5 (Task 18); working tree clean.
- The dev-server reload loop is permanently dead at the script level — any future "bun run dev" is safe. Dev logs: /tmp/rmis-dev.log.

- BUG FOUND & FIXED (pre-existing, exposed by the drawer): React portals bubble synthetic events through the REACT tree — the sheet (and every dialog portal) is a React descendant of AppShell's PullToRefresh container, whose onPointerDown captured the pointer onto the scroller for ANY press (even physically outside it), silently killing every mouse/pen click inside portals below lg (touch immune — pointerType "touch" returns before capture, which is why phones never hit it). Fixed in pull-to-refresh.tsx startPointerPull with a contains(event.target) guard (in-scroller pull behavior byte-identical; the vendored block's gesture is untouched) and belt-and-suspenders stopPropagation on the sheet's portal wrapper.
- Browser verification (agent-browser, 390×844): drawer anatomy matches the reference screenshot-for-screenshot (scrim strip, header circles, chips row with +3, dark Description pill, About/Responsibilities, docked blue CTA, home indicator); tabs switch (Review shows Position summary rows + MQR + Compensation; Company shows agency block); "Apply this job" opens the confirm morph-dialog and Cancel closes it (post-fix); bookmark toggles + persists; back slides the sheet down and lands on the board; card quick-view "View Details" deep-link opens the drawer for the right posting; anonymous drawer shows "Sign in to Apply" and routes to #/signin. Desktop 1440×900 re-verified pixel-identical (typographic column + tabs + summary/agency aside). Console clean on fresh load; bun run lint clean.

Stage Summary:
- Mobile complete detail is now the reference drawer; desktop keeps the exact Workze page; both come from one data/state path. The portal-vs-PullToRefresh pointer-capture bug (mouse-dead dialogs below lg) is dead at the source. Uncommitted in tree for the user's review, per repo convention.

Task ID: 22
Agent: Z.ai Code (main agent)
Task: User report — "take a look at the stepper its not properly aligned" (mobile application progress card screenshot). Fix the reui c-stepper-8 wiring in the ApplicationJourneyCard so the rails connect the circles cleanly.

Work Log:
- Diagnosed from the user's screenshot + zoom: (1) the blue rail tucks UNDER the "2" circle and pokes out ~12px past its right edge; (2) the gray 2→3 rail starts ~40px after the "2" circle (visible stub → gap → line). Root cause: the borrowed c-stepper-8 separator classes (left-[calc(50%+0.875rem)] + w-[calc(100%-2rem+0.225rem)]) presumes CENTERED size-7 indicators; this wiring renders LEFT-ALIGNED indicators on equal flex-1 columns, so the math started each rail mid-column and overshot the next circle.
- applicant-home.tsx JourneyStepper fix (reui port untouched — @/components/reui/stepper still renders every part): separator recalibrated to this layout as a center-to-center rail — `absolute left-3.5 top-[13px] w-full` (from this 28px circle's center to the next column's circle center; top-[13px] centers the 2px line on the 28px circles). Both ends tuck under the opaque circles (bg-primary / bg-background all opaque). DOM order swapped so the rail renders BEFORE the trigger column: both are positioned (li relative + column relative), so the later column paints above the rail and the circles cover the tucked ends on BOTH sides (previously the rail painted over its own circle — invisible over the blue check but would draw a gray line across the blue "2").
- Second defect found while verifying desktop (1280×800): the middle indicator sat 10px lower (cy 536.3 vs 526.3) — the port's StepperNav base is `items-center`, so once the sm+ captions give items different heights the taller li got vertically centered. Fixed with `self-start` on the li (top-align within the nav; no effect on mobile where heights are equal). Port still untouched.
- Verified with agent-browser (390×844 + 1280×800, getBoundingClientRect): mobile — circles cx 42/153.33/264.67 at cy 524.3; separator1 spans 42→153.33, separator2 spans 153.33→264.67 (EXACT center-to-center), both cy = circles' cy; elementFromPoint at all four circle-edge tuck points → indicator (circles cover the rail), mid-gap probes → separator (rail visible). All four stages re-shot: NO.01 Submitted (blue+gray), NO.02 Shortlisted (both blue, green star), NO.03 In Review (blue+gray, spinner), NO.04 Not Shortlisted (both blue, red ×) — every rail connects edge-to-edge, labels left-aligned under circles. Desktop — all cy 526.3, separators center-to-center, captions top-aligned. Screenshots .verify/22-mobile-stepper-final.png, 22-stepper-zoom-final.png, 22-card-{1,2,3}-zoom.png, 22-card-4c-zoom.png, 22-desktop-fixed.png + 22-desktop-zoom.png. profile-view.tsx also uses the reui port but VERTICAL with its own rail — not affected. bun run lint clean; dev.log clean.

Stage Summary:
- The journey stepper rails now run exactly circle-center → circle-center on mobile and desktop, with both ends hidden under the circles (no stubs, no gaps, no lines crossing circles), all indicators on one baseline, and labels consistently left-aligned — across all four pipeline states. The fix is wiring-level class calibration inside JourneyStepper only; the reui stepper port and profile-view's vertical stepper are untouched.

Task ID: 22-c
Agent: Z.ai Code (main agent)
Task: User follow-up — "i also notice that the status submitted is already pointing to under review". A Submitted application must not read as already advancing toward Under Review.

Work Log:
- Root cause: JourneyStepper's controlled value was hardcoded `decided ? 3 : 2` — never 1 — so a Submitted (stage "Applied") application rendered step 1 as completed (check) with the rail to Under Review already blue and circle "2" highlighted.
- applicant-home.tsx JourneyStepper: live step now derives from the actual stage — `const value = decided ? 3 : reviewing ? 2 : 1;` (Applied/Submitted → 1, Under Review → 2, decision → 3), fed to <Stepper value>. The per-item local `state` mirror (drives the inactive indicator classes) now derives from the same value: `index + 1 < value ? completed : index + 1 === value ? active : inactive` + decision override. Doc comment updated. Indicator CONTENT stays port-driven (active step renders its number; completed renders the check; reviewing renders the spinner via the loading flag).
- Verified with agent-browser (390×844 + 1280×800, computed styles + data-states): Submitted card → items [active, inactive, inactive], BOTH rails gray rgb(230,235,243), circles blue "1" / white "2" / white "3" (.verify/22c-mobile-zoom.png, 22c-desktop-zoom.png — desktop centered captions intact); In Review → [completed, active, inactive], rail 1 blue + rail 2 gray, spinner live (.verify/22c-inreview-zoom.png); Shortlisted/Not Selected unchanged ([completed, completed, active], both rails blue, green ★ rgb(31,122,61) / red ✗ rgb(168,67,47)). bun run lint clean; dev.log clean.

Task ID: 25
Agent: WorkBuddy (UiDesigner)
Task: User directive — "review existing UI design and propose optimizations", then "you can proceed implementing your proposal". Full design audit of RMIS v4.2 (112 components, 13 views) with measured evidence, followed by implementation of the P0/P1 foundations and three P2 items.

Work Log:
- AUDIT (`design-review.html`, repo root): contrast computed with WCAG 2.1 relative luminance over the actual token pairs, call-site counts via ripgrep. Headline findings: (1) `#1591dc` carrying white text = 3.43:1 — every primary CTA and every brand link failed AA, while its own hover shade `#0e7abf` passed at 4.61, i.e. only the RESTING state was broken; (2) the `@theme` type scale (12/16/23/33/44/72) was documentation only — the UI used 125 arbitrary `text-[Npx]` declarations across 13 sizes, eight of them used exactly once, including the Data Privacy Act footer at 10px/4.28; (3) `--radius-md/lg/xl/2xl` all resolved to 16px, so fields and cards shared one silhouette across 143 call sites; (4) two button systems coexisted (cva capsule 36px + shadow vs the canonical 44px/12px/flat) and `sectionCtaBtn` was 36px on phones / 44px on desktop — the smaller target on the touch device.
- F1 brand role split (globals.css): added `--brand-50/500/600/700`; `--primary` → brand-600 (#0b6fa8, 5.45:1), `--primary-hover` → brand-700 (#0a6295, 6.57:1), `--ring` stays brand-500 (a ring is a graphic, 3.43 ≥ 3:1). `.dlg-cta` fill moved to brand-600. DARK MODE inverts the INK rather than the fill (white on #1591dc is 3.43, but near-black #08222f on it is 5.04 and a bright fill is what reads on graphite); the text-role blue lightens to #63b4ea/#8ccbf2 because brand-600 as ink on a dark surface is 3.09. `.chip-ink` → brand-600; monogram gradient deepened (every stop ≥5.4 with white).
- F2 type ladder: eight steps, one role each (11 label caps-only / 12 caption / 13 meta / 15 ui / 16 body / 18 card-heading / 24 display / 32 view-display / 44 hero). `PageHeader` title is `text-display` (24) at EVERY width — it used to grow to 28, which put the page title below the 33px "card heading" the tokens reserved. 19 × `text-[10px]` → 11px labels or `text-caption` prose; footer legal 10px/4.28 → 12px `text-stone`/6.30.
- F3 radius contract: `--radius-control: 10px` on `.dlg-input`, `.dlg-cta`, `.dlg-cta-ok/bad`, `.dlg-ghost`, `.dlg-ghost-dashed` + the button presets. The `rounded-*` aliases were NOT remapped (rounded-md is used on 20px checkboxes, where 10px would round a square into a circle) — the aliasing is documented as "surface step, three names for one value" instead.
- F4 one button system: `ui/button.tsx`'s `buttonVariants` re-pointed to the canonical recipe (10px, 44px, flat, no hover lift, brand-600) so the two vendored consumers (alert-dialog, upload) stop speaking a second language. `sectionCtaBtn`/`sectionGhostBtn` now key on the POINTER, not the viewport: 44px default, 40px under `[@media(pointer:fine)]`. New `.tap-44` grows the hit area of 36px/32px controls to 44px on coarse pointers with zero layout movement.
- F5 hierarchy: `FilterChip` selected state is now ink (`bg-foreground text-background`) instead of a solid brand capsule with `shadow-cta` — a filter is a lens, not a commitment, and the brand fill belongs to actions. 40px + tap-44. (First attempt used `bg-ink text-white`, which broke in dark mode — `--ink` flips to moonlight there; the foreground/background pair inverts correctly. Caught in the dark-mode screenshot.)
- F6 landing hero (public-landing.tsx): the band was a full-bleed gradient whose only child was an H1 restating the header's own nav label. It now carries the headline (`text-view-display`, 32), two live facts ("N open", "N closing this week" from the same `deadlineState()` the cards use) and the search + sort controls; the register starts on screen one. Counts are gated on `openJobs.length > 0`, so the loading/error/empty states still degrade to the bare band.
- F7/F8 + responsive fix: `command-center.tsx` active-recruitment rows now drop the deadline + count to their own line below sm — on a 390px phone the shrink-0 trailing meta had been squeezing the job title down to a single letter ("A.").
- NOT DONE (deliberate, recorded in the doc): staff mobile nav 7 → 4 + More (needs an IA decision on which destinations survive), the 51-eyebrow cull (editorial, per view), dark-mode hex migration (46 literals, needs per-component visual checks), two-greens consolidation (touches charts + category cards). Corrected two claims from the first draft of the review after reading the code: `.chip-blue` already used `--info` (never broken) and `StageDot` already renders a glyph (not colour-only).
- Verified in a real browser (Playwright + installed Chrome, 1440×900 and 390×844, against a COPY of db/production-data.db): CTA 3.43 → 5.45; brand link on Cloud Card 3.00 → 4.76; footer 12px/6.30; dark CTA 5.04; page title 24 at both widths; 0px horizontal overflow on every audited page; filter chip inverts correctly in dark; mobile job title no longer truncates. `next build` clean, `eslint` clean on every touched file, `tsc --noEmit` 27 errors all pre-existing (Prisma/script types) and none in a touched file. No protected file touched — `notify.ts`, the middleware relay, the email callers and the `.env` email block are untouched.

Stage Summary:
- The four foundations the product was leaking through are now closed: brand blue is a two-role system (graphics vs. readable) that passes AA in both themes, the type scale is real in code rather than in comments, controls have their own radius, and one button anatomy exists. Every primary CTA in RMIS went from 3.43:1 to 5.45:1 without a visible rebrand, and the public landing's first screen is now action instead of a heading.

Task ID: 25 (continued)
Agent: WorkBuddy (UiDesigner)
Task: User — "continue with implementing your proposal". Second wave: the items the first pass deliberately deferred (F7 staff mobile nav, F8 green consolidation, touch-target sweep), plus doc + worklog updates.

Work Log:
- F7 STAFF MOBILE NAV (mobile-tab-bar.tsx) — the 7-tab bar is gone. Added a module-level `STAFF_PRIMARY` map (ADMIN: operations/recruitment/review-queue; EVALUATOR: review-queue/candidates/recruitment) chosen by daily frequency rather than rail order, so re-prioritising is a two-array edit. Staff now render three destinations + "More" (MoreHorizontal). The overflow list is derived (`staffOverflow`, not memoized — react-hooks/preserve-manual-memoization rejected the useMemo chain, and the array only feeds a row list). The More panel reuses MenuRow and carries: overflow destinations → Find (⌘K) → hairline divider → identity card (avatar + name + email + role pill) → Sign out in --bad. The Account tab is deleted; `accountPanel`/`accountTab`/`hasAccountTab`/`CircleUserRound` removed with it. `restingId` now falls back to "more" when the active view lives in the sheet, so the "you are here" word pill survives — verified on #/analytics. `handleValueChange` treats "more" like "account" (open the panel, never navigate). Nav-tab content is now `open === e.id` (was `sectionPanels && open === e.id`, which would have starved staff tabs of their panel). Tab-count-dependent `MIRROR_BAR_CLASS[n]` now always resolves to the tested 4-tab entry; `roomy` is true for every bar, so the applicant's +18px per-gap tuning applies to staff too. File header rewritten — the old 70-line comment documented the 7-tab machine and would have lied to the next reader.
- F8 ONE GREEN FAMILY (globals.css) — `--viz-2`, `--chart-2` (both themes), `--color-recovery-green` and `--color-emerald-accent` collapsed onto #2f9e57. Rationale: the old #31ce01 measured 2.11:1 on white — below the 3:1 that any chart mark owes its canvas (WCAG 1.4.11) — and #34c771 could never hold a label (2.20 with white). #2f9e57 is 3.42:1, sits in the same family as --ok, and stays distinct from the gold/lilac/coral series. Palette header comment updated to match.
- TOUCH SWEEP — `.tap-44` (coarse-pointer hit-area expansion, zero layout movement) applied to every remaining 36px control: onboarding-tour (3), candidates (2), job-posting-detail, personal-info-section (2), review-queue row actions (2), review-workspace (2), settings (2), plus PageHeader's back link and the two 36px ghost buttons in ui/shell.tsx. 17 sites total.
- DOC (design-review.html) — "What shipped" extended with rows 9–11 (nav / green / touch sweep) and three more measured rows; F7 and F8 each gained a Shipped block (nav mapping table, green rationale); the "deliberately not done" list re-scoped to the eyebrow cull, the dark-mode hex migration and the ~60 remaining arbitrary type sizes; roadmap intro updated. Embedded the admin More-sheet screenshot (self-contained data URI, doc now 259KB).
- Verified: `admin tabs: ["Dashboard","Jobs","Review","More"]` at 390px, bar 300px wide (was ~351 for 7 tabs); More panel exposes Users/Analytics/Settings/Find/Sign out; clicking Analytics navigates to #/analytics and the More tab reads as the active one; applicant bar unchanged (`["Home","Jobs","Profile","Settings"]`, screenshot with the tour dismissed via localStorage). Analytics funnel + donut confirmed painting #2f9e57. `next build` clean, eslint clean on every touched file.
- Ops note: a killed `next build` leaves `.next/lock`, and the next build then hangs silently for 20+ minutes instead of erroring. `rm -f .next/lock` before retrying. (Hit twice.)

Stage Summary:
- Every roadmap item except the dark-mode hex migration is now shipped. The phone experience for staff is four real targets instead of seven clipped ones with everything else one tap deeper, the charts use one legible green, and no interactive control in the product is smaller than 44px to a finger.

Header comment documents the pattern provenance.
- BUG FOUND & FIXED during verification (pre-existing, not from this task): lib/validation.ts UPLOAD_ACCEPT was "." + EXTENSIONS.join(".") → one comma-less token ".png.jpg.…"; UploadCN parseAccept splits on "," ONLY → matchesAccept failed for EVERY file → all client-side uploads were rejected with "File type not allowed" (they never left the browser; server backstop was fine since it uses the array). Fixed to EXTENSIONS.map(e=>`.${e}`).join(",") with an explanatory comment.
- Browser-verified (testapplicant, desktop 1650×950): preview row shows the red PDF tile + name + "SUPPORTING · 1 KB · Sep 30, 2026" + eye; eye AND name click open the PDF preview modal (inline iframe); Edit Profile → checkbox/PDF tile/eye/trash row + FolderUpload right column; injected 3 files via DataTransfer (pdf, docx, jpg) → all 3 uploaded (after the accept fix; jpg was rejected pre-fix), folder front shows "3 files", tiles render JPG purple / DOCX blue / PDF red; checkbox×3 → Delete selected → confirm dialog → "3 documents deleted", seeded cert remains; Done Editing restores read-only. iPhone 14: section 7 row renders with clean ellipsis, eye opens the modal. Zero page/console errors; dev.log all 200s. NOTE: agent-browser pointer clicks don't reach React handlers under iPhone-14 touch emulation (native el.click() via eval works; desktop clicks fine) — test-tooling quirk, not an app bug; also reconfirmed the pre-existing Character-Reference forward gate on section 01.

- Rigorous field-by-field diff (bun:sqlite, all Position + JobPosting columns) found exactly ONE dropped datum: the old Position.placeOfAssignment values were never mapped to the Task-25 Position.section field (all 7 live rows had section=null). The uploaded legacy PlaceOfAssignment lookup table (Accounting Unit / HR Development Unit / Metrology Laboratory / Materials and Corrosion Laboratory) is legacy dropdown cruft — the real per-position values live on Position.placeOfAssignment and were used as the mapping source.
- Backfilled Position.section from the uploaded placeOfAssignment keyed by the unique plantilla itemNumber (transactional, pre-backup db/production-data.pre-section-backfill-20261021-0950.db): AO II (HRMO I) + AO V (Cashier III) → Human Resource Management and Administrative Services Section; AO II (Financial Analyst I) → Financial Management Section; Engineer I + Senior SRS → Facilities and Equipment Maintenance and Operations Support Section; SRS II → Advanced Mechatronics and Robotics Section; Supervising SRS → Physical Measurement Section.

Cookies survive in the agent-browser daemon, so per-call: restart server → reload → operate.

Zero page errors.

Work Log:
- Discovered the Save-as-later BACKEND was already fully in place (from the earlier draft batch): jobCreateSchema accepts `status: "PENDING" | "PUBLISHED"`, POST /api/jobs maps Save-as-later → PENDING with an audit note, PATCH /api/jobs/[id] flips status either way (its comment literally anticipates "the table's status dropdown PATCHes this key"), GET hides PENDING+overdue from non-staff, and JobWire.status ships to clients. Only the FRONTEND was missing.
- (1) CREATE DIALOG (recruitment.tsx JobFormDialog): footer in create mode is now [Cancel] [Save as later] [Create & publish] — Save as later is a ghost button POSTing status PENDING with toast "Job posting saved as pending — It stays off the public job board until you publish it from the jobs table."; busy state became busyMode ("publish" | "later" | "edit") so only the clicked action shows loading and the others disable; header description + footer hint rewritten to explain both paths; edit mode unchanged (Cancel / Save changes, status never touched by edits).
- (2) TABLE DROPDOWN (recruitment.tsx): new per-row PublicationDropdown in the Status column (desktop 160px + mobile 140px, stacked above the deadline-ladder pill which now renders ONLY for PUBLISHED rows — a "Closing in 3 days" badge on an off-board draft would contradict the Pending state). Portal-based shadcn DropdownMenu because the motion/table block's overflow-hidden + overflow-auto wrappers would clip the in-field motion Select. Trigger = compact select-look (h-8/text-xs desktop, h-7/text-[11px] mobile) reading "Published"/"Pending"; items carry a check mark + "live on the board"/"off the board" hints. onStatusChange flips optimistically, PATCHes { status }, toasts ("Job posting published" / "Job posting moved to pending"), silently re-fetches server truth, and reverts on failure — same contract as the inline vacancy editor. Toolbar status filter extended with Published / Pending lenses (Open/Closed now also require PUBLISHED so drafts never leak into the deadline lenses).
- (3) OPEN-PROFILE REMOVAL (review-queue.tsx): deleted the hover-reveal UserRound icon from the kanban cards (KanbanCardBody action prop gone, group/card coupling dropped) and the icon button from the desktop list action cell (column width 150→118px, single Review/View capsule remains — matching the mobile column). navigate + iconBtn + the already-dead ghostBtn imports removed; header + cell comments updated. UserRound stays (list header IconChip). The attached upload/production-data.db (09:27) was verified as the SAME file Task 30 already adopted — live DB re-checked: the 7 real production jobs intact, all PUBLISHED.
- (4) CONTACT MODAL WIDTH (contact-requests.tsx): sm:max-w-xl → sm:max-w-2xl lg:max-w-3xl (672px laptops / 768px desktop; phones stay viewport-fitted).
- Browser-verified end-to-end (agent-browser, session rmis, desktop 1650×950 + iPhone 14): create modal shows the three-button footer → filled title + Save as later → toast fired, modal closed, table gained a "Pending" row with NO ladder pill; toolbar Pending lens isolated exactly that row ("1 total"); per-row menu → Published → toast + pill returned ("Published · Open until filled"); menu → Pending again → toast, pill hidden; anonymous landing after cookies-clear did NOT list the pending draft while real jobs rendered; test job then DELETEd via /api/jobs/8 (DB back to exactly the 7 production jobs). Review Queue: 0 "Open profile" buttons in list AND kanban (4 cards render clean), Review/View buttons intact on both. Contact modal measured 768×874 desktop (was 576) and 358px on iPhone. Mobile jobs table: 7 compact dropdowns, 28px, zero horizontal overflow, menu opens with both items. Edit modal footer unchanged. bun run lint clean; dev.log all 200s; fresh-reload console: 0 errors (an earlier job-posting-detail.tsx console line proved stale — fresh session shows nothing).

Agent: Z.ai Code (main orchestrator)

Result: both lists end on ONE shared visible line 24px above the fold and still grow/shrink with the screen. The old 100dvh − 13.5rem caps are gone (superseded); all comments updated.
- "Complete Your Profile" banner RETIRED: the whole `{!profileComplete && …}` block deleted (it rendered at the page bottom on both desktop and mobile); the OnboardingTour step anchored to [data-tour="profile-banner"] removed with it (tour is now 2 steps, Apply Now first); AlertCircle import dropped; profileComplete stays — the mini profile ring + metric remain the completion nudge.
- Verified (agent-browser, session rmis, testapplicant): 1650×950 — grid 104→926 (h 822), left list 510→926 (h 417, scrollH 805), right list 366→926 (h 561, scrollH 1260), bottomDelta 0, scrollTop settable on both, banner absent. 1650×750 — gridH 622, both bottoms 726, delta 0. 1650×1100 — delta 0, lists grew (567/711). iPhone 14 — mobile list maxHeight none, overflow visible, flex 0 1 auto, 4 cards in natural flow, banner gone. Interactions: application card → detail modal → exact-name Close closes; bookmark card's inner "View …" button → #/job-opportunities?job=5 deep-link (note: BoardJobCard's handler is the inner summary button, not the article root — programmatic article.click() is a no-op by design). Work Log:
- VENDORED beui motion/drawer (registry CLI unreachable — `bunx shadcn add @beui/drawer` timed out after 120 s): src/components/motion/drawer.tsx written VERBATIM from the user's source. Transitive imports checked one-by-one (the Task 20-era lesson): @/lib/ease (EASE_OUT + SPRING_PANEL ✓ exist), @/lib/presence-gate ✓, @/lib/utils cn ✓, motion/react ✓ (motion ^14.1.0) — but @/lib/hooks/use-focus-trap did NOT exist → wrote src/lib/hooks/use-focus-trap.ts to the beui contract (focus into panel on open — container itself when tabindex present, else first visible focusable; Tab/Shift+Tab wrap; Escape → onEscape; restore opener focus on release). Two hardening choices: onEscape mirrored through a ref so the effect deps stay [active] (the Drawer passes an inline opts object — deps-included would refocus the opener on every parent render), and focus restore only fires while focus is still inside the container (drawer→modal handoff never yanks focus).

The rail's return became a fragment: section + <ApplicationsHistoryDrawer> (each rail copy owns its instance; only the visible copy's button is reachable). The drawer: beui Drawer side="right" sm:w-96, ariaLabel "Application history"; header (eyebrow + "Application History" + "N applications on record" + round X), flex-1 scroll-thin list of ALL applications newest-first (rail list arrives pre-sorted) — each row: title / truncated place / "Applied <date>" + the SAME StatusPill treatment as journey cards (regular weight, In Review keeps its pulse spinner), divide-y rows with hover:bg-fog/60; footer hint "Select an application to view its full posting and journey." Row click → drawer closes + onOpen(app) opens the SAME detail modal the journey cards use (Cancel Application stays in one place).
- Verified (agent-browser, session rmis, testapplicant, 4 seeded applications): desktop 1650×950 — View All in header, drawer 384px right-aligned, 4 rows (Engineer I/Not Selected, AO II HRMO/Submitted, SRS II/In Review + spinner, AO V Cashier/Submitted), count "4 applications on record", body overflow locked; Escape closes + body unlocks; X closes; backdrop click closes; row click → drawer gone + detail modal ("Engineer I") opens → exact-name Close closes. iPhone 14 — mobile rail header shows View All, drawer 320px ≤ 85vw, scrim + blur visible, 4 rows, row click → modal opens; bottom bar correctly UNDER the full-height panel. Agent: Z.ai Code (main orchestrator)
Task: user, four items: (1) mobile drawer header ("Your Applications / Application History / N on record") still kinda big — decrease padding/spacing/height, mobile only; (2) applicant home vector hero's "View All Jobs" button gets the text cascade animation, label unchanged, every 5 s; (3) mini profile: remove the Edit Profile button, replace with the reui c-tooltip-5 tooltip ("bunx shadcn add @reui/c-tooltip-5" + pattern source supplied) configured to tell the user to complete his profile; (4) desktop home: decrease the right-side column (mini profile + application progress) width a little.

Work Log:
- (1) DRAWER HEADER tightened again on phones only: container padding 16/12/16 → 12/10/14 (px-3.5 pb-2.5 pt-3), title text-base → text-sm (14px) with the mt-0.5 dropped on mobile, meta text-[11px] → text-[10px], close button 32 → 28px with a 14px glyph; every value restores at sm: (20/16/20, 18px, 12px, 36px). Measured: iPhone 14 header 12/10/14 + 14px + 10px + 28px (block ~77px tall, was ~90); desktop unchanged 20/16/20 + 18px + 12px + 36px.
- (2) CASCADE: the beui TextCascade's engine (ActionSwapText animation="cascade", already vendored + used by the site header) now wraps BOTH hero pills' labels (desktop vector hero + phone banner twin, shared beat). TextCascade only cascades when its text CHANGES, so the label stays "View All Jobs" and a 5 s window.setInterval flips a cascadeTick counter that feeds the ActionSwapText `value` — re-keying the letter layers rolls them out/up and back in on the 5 s beat (reduced-motion users get the static label via ActionSwapText's own fallback). Verified by sampling letter transforms across the 5 s boundary: settled (none/1) → exiting (translateY(-105%)/0) → re-entering.
- (3) TOOLTIP: registry CLI timed out (120 s) but reui.io's registry JSON answered via curl: c-tooltip-5 = example block + deps [@reui/badge, button, tooltip]; tooltip is stock shadcn/Radix and @radix-ui/react-tooltip@1.4.1 was ALREADY in the dependency tree → wrote src/components/ui/tooltip.tsx as the standard Radix Tooltip/TooltipTrigger/TooltipContent/TooltipProvider wrapper styled on the house popover recipe (popover surface, hairline border, system shadow, animate-in, portal + arrow). @/components/reui/badge already existed (earlier registry port) with the destructive/success variants — reused as-is. The mini profile's Edit Profile capsule is REPLACED by the c-tooltip-5 anatomy: round hairline icon button (the drawer-close anatomy, size-9, desktop-only like the capsule it replaced) wearing an Info icon + destructive %-badge (hidden until the quiet completion fetch lands), opening a w-64 content card: "Profile Setup" + {ringPct}% done badge (success "Complete" when done), the missing §7.4 sections as bullets (PROFILE_SECTIONS ordered to computeProfileCompletion's sectionDone — new profileGaps state filled by the existing fetch: personal/education/work/training/eligibility/awards/documents), and the underlined "Complete profile →" link (Go to profile when complete); hover previews, click navigates to the Profile page (verified #/profile).
- (4) GRID: desktop top row lg:grid-cols-12 + col-span-7/5 → lg:grid-cols-[7.5fr_4.5fr] with col-span-1 each — right column 41.7% → 37.5% (1650px: 644→487px; 1280px: ~416→348px), the requested "a little"; both column comments updated.
- Verified (agent-browser, session rmis, testapplicant): desktop 1650×950 — grid 811.25/486.75, Edit Profile gone, info button visible with 0% badge, tooltip shows all 7 missing sections + link, click-through lands on #/profile, cascade letters animate on the 5 s beat; 1280×800 — no horizontal overflow, 580/348 split. iPhone 14 — drawer header 12/10/14 + 14px + 10px + 28px, banner cascade present, tooltip trigger correctly display:none (desktop-only affordance). One test-automation note: "View All" find-by-name clicks the DOM-FIRST (desktop) rail copy — on phones it opens the drawer inside the hidden lg:grid; the visible mobile copy needs an offsetParent-filtered click (automation-only quirk, no product impact). bun run lint clean; console clean; dev.log clean; errors buffer only the 4 known empty HMR ✗ rows. Task ID: 60 (follow-up to 59 — evaluator review modal: stepper scope, dialog focus steal, duplicate Direct email card; earlier same-ID entries unrelated)
Agent: Z.ai Code (main orchestrator)
Task: user — "(1) on the evaluator review queue there is an 'Appointment' on the stepper our system should be up to shortlisted only so remove that (2) on the review queue modal forms — Send Interview Invitation / Send Skills-Exam Notice and others — moving to other fields automatically brings me back to the first field every time I type (3) remove the 'Direct email' card (recipient, Up to 3 attachments · 5 MB each, Send a direct email, History) — the Send Email button already exists at the modal header below Full profile". Mid-task follow-up: "lets remove the effect we have when opening the modals on our system" — kill the open-time auto-focus entirely rather than merely re-key it.

Work Log:
- FOCUS-STEAL ROOT CAUSE (src/components/motion/center-morph-modal.tsx): CenterMorphModalContent's open-state effect ran on `[context, dismissible]` deps, but the provider memoizes context over `setOpen`, which is rebuilt whenever a caller passes an INLINE `onOpenChange` arrow — i.e. on every parent re-render. Every keystroke in NoticeFormDialog / DirectEmailDialog / DecisionDialog (all local state + inline arrows) therefore re-ran the effect, whose requestAnimationFrame focus frame re-focused the panel's FIRST focusable element — yanking the caret out of the field being typed (and popping the mobile keyboard on open).
- FIX 1 (user direction): the open-time auto-focus is REMOVED — the focus frame and its cancel are gone; opening a modal leaves focus where it was (no caret grab, no keyboard pop). The effect now only locks body scroll and wires Esc/Tab handling, keyed to `[open, dismissible]` (the open TRANSITION), with the live context read through contextRef (setOpen for Escape, triggerId for the close-time focus restore — kept, it only runs on close and is a no-op for programmatic dialogs).
- FIX 2 (discovered in verification, same block): the scroll lock's snapshot-restore deadlocked with stacked modals — every layer snapshots "hidden" set by the layer beneath, and when two layers close in the same commit (both listen for Escape on window) the inner restore can land after the outer one, wedging body.overflow=hidden with zero dialogs open (observed live: dialogs 0, overflow hidden). Replaced with a module-level reference count (`openModalCount`): lock sets hidden, cleanup decrements and clears ONLY when the last modal unlocks.
- FIX 3 (src/components/views/review-workspace.tsx): successPath loses its 4th node — the stepper is now Applied → Under Review → Shortlisted (system terminal stage); Rejected path unchanged (Applied → Under Review → terminal red node). CalendarCheck import removed.
- FIX 4 (same file): the rail's DirectEmailCard component, its parseAttachments/emailStatusVariant helpers, the `emails` state, the GET /email fetch in load(), and the DirectEmailRow type are removed — the header card's "Send Email" button opens the SAME DirectEmailDialog (kept verbatim). Orphaned imports dropped (Send, timeAgo, type StatusVariant). Rail now ends at Notices; one fewer API request per workspace open.
- VERIFIED (agent-browser, session rmis, testevaluator@mirdc.gov.ph): desktop 1650×950 + iPhone 14 — stepper renders exactly 3 nodes on both (Juan Under Review + Maria Shortlisted); directEmail/attachments/history strings absent from the modal on both viewports; Send Email composer opens, Subject→Message typing keeps focus in each field per-keystroke (activeElement checked via eval), full send flow works (StatefulButton validate → Radix confirm → POST /email 200 → toast → refetch; audit DIRECT_EMAIL_SENT mock); interview invitation dialog: no focus on open (activeElement stays BUTTON), full Venue + HR contact typing stays put, Esc + Cancel close correctly. Scroll lock: stacked modal+dialog → orderly close keeps lock then unlocks on last close; simultaneous double-Escape close unlocks immediately (was the wedge). bun run lint clean; dev.log all 2xx.
- TAR SYNC (housekeeping, same policy as Tasks 52/57/58/59): rebuilt rmis-v4.3-2026-10-21.tar.gz from /tmp staging (same include/exclude policy), verified Task 60 markers inside the tarred files, promoted to root + download/ with md5-identical copies.
- Screenshots (tool-results/): t60-stepper-desktop.png, t60-review-modal-desktop.png, t60-stepper-mobile.png, t60-review-modal-mobile.png, t60-invite-dialog-mobile.png.

Stage Summary:
- The evaluator review modal now ends its stepper at Shortlisted, carries no duplicate Direct email card (the header's Send Email button remains the single composer door), and its dialogs no longer fight the user for the caret: the open-time auto-focus effect is gone from the official modal block, the effect keys to the open transition instead of per-keystroke context churn, and the body scroll lock is reference-counted so stacked/simultaneous closes can never wedge scrolling again.

Task ID: 61 (follow-up to 60 — applicant home journey cards on thin screens, CSC eligibility rank in the MQR gate, PDS row auto-collapse; earlier same-ID entries unrelated)
Agent: Z.ai Code (main orchestrator)
Task: user — "(1) the application progress status cards on mobile view — make it a little bit compact in mobile that has a thin screen (2) 'Career Service Professional (Second Level)' is higher than 'Career Service Sub-Professional (First Level)', so if the job requires First Level and the applicant holds Second Level he should be qualified to apply (3) after the PDS has been extracted, make the PDS dropdown auto-close after 3 seconds instead of letting it hang — the applicant can open it anytime".

Work Log:
- COMPACT JOURNEY CARDS (src/components/views/applicant-home.tsx): JourneyStepper gains a thin-screen pass — the decorative "STEP N" eyebrow is now sm+-only (a whole extra row per step on phones for zero information the state badge doesn't carry), the trigger column's gap tightens gap-2→gap-1.5 and the inner title/badge column gap-1→gap-0.5, and StepperNav's inter-step gap narrows gap-3→gap-2 on phones (rail geometry keys off the unchanged size-6 indicator, so rails still tuck under the next opaque circle). ApplicationJourneyCard's place line mt-1.5→mt-1 and the Next Step divider block mt-2/pt-2→mt-1.5/pt-1.5 on phones; the rail list spacing space-y-4→space-y-3 on phones. sm+ keeps the full c-stepper-7 anatomy verbatim (verified: 12 eyebrows visible on desktop across 4 cards).
- ELIGIBILITY RANK (src/lib/mqr.ts — the apply gate used by /api/jobs/verify-mqr AND /api/jobs/apply): the eligibility dimension now matches by CSC RANK, not raw tokens. Root cause of the bug: requirement "Career Service Sub-Professional (First Level)" tokenizes (after ELIGIBILITY_STOPWORDS) to ["sub","first"] while a "Career Service Professional (Second Level)" holder's title tokenizes to ZERO tokens (professional/second/level are all stopwords) — the legacy subset rule failed, blocking a higher-ranked applicant. New eligibilityGrade() ladder: RA 1080 / Bar-Board = 5, Professional (Second Level, non-sub) = 5, Sub-Professional (First Level) = 2, named grants (Honor Graduate etc.) = 0 with the legacy token-subset fallback. A First-Level requirement accepts grade >= 2 (Professional qualifies — the user's exact scenario); a Second-Level requirement accepts only grade >= 5 (Sub-Professional does not — mirrors the reviewer-side requirements.ts report, which already ranked this way). Behavior notes: requirement "Career Service Professional (Second Level)" was previously satisfied by ANY eligibility (token-empty boilerplate branch) — now properly gated to Second-Level-or-higher holders, in lockstep with the evaluator requirements report.
- UNIT-VERIFIED the ladder with bun against verifyMqr: Sub-Prof req ← Professional holder MEETS (user's scenario), Prof req ← Professional MEETS, Prof req ← Sub holder FAILS, Sub-Prof req ← Sub holder MEETS, Honor Grad named req ← HG holder MEETS, Sub-Prof req ← RA1080 holder MEETS (6/6). LIVE-VERIFIED via POST /api/jobs/verify-mqr on real postings 3 and 5 (Second-Level + RA1080 wording): allMet=true, eligibility MEETS.
- PDS AUTO-CLOSE (src/components/views/profile/pds-upload-card.tsx): after a SUCCESSFUL extraction+auto-apply (onSuccess, following toast + onProfileReplaced + session refresh), a 3-second timer collapses the "Auto-fill from PDS" collapsible instead of leaving it hanging open. Manual toggling cancels the pending timer via the Collapsible onOpenChange wrapper (re-opening must never be yanked shut); unmount clears it; error paths never schedule one (the Try-Again alert must stay visible). The extraction summary + one-extraction lock strip persist in state/DB, so the row re-opens anytime with the full result.
- VERIFIED (agent-browser, session rmis, testapplicant): iPhone 14 + fresh reloads — journey cards render with 0 STEP eyebrows and tight rhythm on both iPhone 14 and a 360px thin viewport (screenshot t61-journey-360.png), desktop 1650×950 keeps STEP 1/2/3 + badges (t61-journey-desktop.png). PDS flow end-to-end twice (Clear Forms & Re-upload → confirm → re-upload the applicant's own known-good xlsx fixture upload/6/…xlsx): in-page 150ms-interval state recorder timestamps the run — open:idle → uploading +4.65s → extracting +4.80s → applied +5.72s (row OPEN, summary visible) → row COLLAPSED +9.00s = 3278ms window ≈ exactly the 3-second spec; manual re-open persists 4s+ (timer cancelled), lock strip present after. bun run lint clean; dev.log all 2xx for the session (the handful of 401s are the deliberate sign-out/sign-in probes).
- DEMO-STATE RESTORE (housekeeping within the same pass): the verification's Clear Forms cycle reset testapplicant's isProfileComplete flag (the §7.4 gate then bounced Home→Profile with its toast — initially mistaken for a nav bug); restored via the page's own "Mark Profile as Complete" confirm (isProfileComplete=true again). testapplicant's profile ends auto-filled from the SAME fixture it already carried pre-task (the PDS owner's data — Mar James Kenitte Delimios), references intact.
- Screenshots (tool-results/): t61-journey-mobile.png, t61-journey-360.png, t61-journey-desktop.png, t61-pds-locked.png.

Stage Summary:
- The applicant home's progress cards are compact on thin phones (eyebrow row dropped, gaps tightened) while desktop keeps the full stepper anatomy; the MQR apply gate now honors the CSC eligibility ladder (Second Level satisfies a First-Level requirement, not vice versa) at both the pre-check endpoint and the server-side apply gate; and the PDS auto-fill row collapses itself 3 seconds after a successful extraction, cancelling politely if the applicant re-opens it.

Task ID: 70
Agent: Z.ai Code (main)
Task: Bug fix — clearing the Key Competency (and any other optional field) in the evaluator job-edit form did not persist on "Save Changes".

Work Log:
- Root cause (two-part contract bug): the dialog built the PATCH body with `keyCompetency: keyCompetency || undefined` (and `opt()` returned undefined for ""), so JSON.stringify DROPPED every cleared key from the request; the server's PATCH treats an absent key as "don't touch", so the old value survived. Same defect silently affected ALL clearable fields: positionType, itemNumber, salaryGrade/Amount, division, section, education/experience/training/eligibility/license, brief/compensation/otherQuals (plain+HTML), and the three dates.
- Frontend fix (src/components/views/recruitment.tsx): `opt()` now returns explicit null for cleared fields; salaryAmount, the six description/comp companion fields, keyCompetency and the three dates send null instead of undefined. Contract is now "null = clear it, absent = untouched". Status stays undefined on edit (table dropdown owns that flip) and numberOfVacancy already sent null.
- Backend hardening (src/app/api/jobs/[id]/route.ts): the Zod schema already accepted null and sanitizeHtml is null-safe, so no validation change. Added `vitalsMeaningful` guard so an unlinked job whose vitals are all null no longer spawns an all-null debris Position row (explicit nulls made that branch fire on every edit); clearing columns on an EXISTING linked position still writes nulls — that is the deletion path being fixed.
- Browser-verified end-to-end (agent-browser, admin@mirdc.dost.gov.ph — ADMIN passes requireEvaluatorFromReq): created throwaway "QA Task70 Temp Posting" via Save as later (PENDING, off the public board) with Key Competency text → Edit → select-all+delete in the rich editor → Save changes → reopened dialog shows EMPTY pane with placeholder; DB position.keyCompetency = NULL. Second path: Section set to "QA Division X" → saved (DB shows value) → cleared → saved → DB section = NULL. PATCH /api/jobs/9 returned 200 both times.
- Regression check: opened Edit on real posting "Administrative Officer II (Financial Analyst I)" — ALL fields prefill (division FAD, item no., SG-11, salary 31705, dates, all four editors) so a full-form save re-sends displayed values and cannot wipe anything the form didn't show; cancelled WITHOUT saving. Status-dropdown and inline-vacancy PATCH callers build their own bodies (untouched).
- Cleanup: temp posting deleted via the UI's Delete permanently flow; orphaned test Position row and the 2 probe audit rows (JOB_POSTING_CREATED/DELETED, entityId 9) removed with explicit DATABASE_URL; sandbox DB back to pristine Task-68 shape (7 postings / 7 positions / 7 users, zero probe rows).
- Tar rebuilt under the established policy (excl. node_modules/.next/.git/dev.log/**.zscripts/dev.log**/tool-results/agent-ctx/download/tarballs): file list byte-identical to the Task 69 artifact (1944 entries, ~53MB), 4 "Task 70" markers verified inside the shipped copies, DB inside tar clean (0 temp jobs, 0 probe audits, 7/7). Promoted to root + download/, md5-identical e0292b89cf3f043faf689ed848ea54d9.

Stage Summary:
- Deleting the Key Competency now persists on Save Changes — and so does clearing ANY optional field on the job form (the reported bug was one instance of a systemic undefined-vs-null serialization flaw). Server contract: explicit null clears, absent key untouched. Deployment tar refreshed (e0292b89…) with the fix; sandbox DB left in production shape.

Task ID: 73
Agent: Z.ai Code (main)
Task: Production incident — uploaded documents recorded in DB but files 404 in the review queue ("Resume-Tebelin_Robert_Jr_C.pdf · SUPPORTING · 95 KB · Oct 22, 2026"); Copilot on the server found only 4 of 70 document records with files at the standalone stored paths; user asked where the original upload storage/backup lives. Also reported: only xlsx "viewable" in review, PDFs/images not viewable.

Work Log:
- Root cause proven in code: every version since v4.1 resolved uploads against `path.join(process.cwd(), "upload")` (documents POST, documents/[id] DELETE, extract, files/[...path] serving). Production runs `node .next/standalone/server.js`, whose entrypoint does `process.chdir(__dirname)` → cwd = `.next/standalone`, a tree `next build` REPLACES. Every rebuild destroyed all uploaded files while Document rows survived in the persistent db/production-data.db. The `UPLOAD_DIR` line carried in production .env since v4.1 was DEAD config — never read by any version (verified against RMISv4.2-Latest@f514141: identical cwd-based code). The Oct 22 review-queue 404s and "only xlsx shows anything" symptom (xlsx has no inline preview → static download card renders without the file; PDF/image renderers hit the 404) are the same incident.
- Recovery answer for the server: no backup exists in the sandbox (upload/ holds only user-uploaded artifacts); remaining sources are (1) files still inside `rmis-latestversion/.next/standalone/upload/` (the 4 survivors), (2) the OLD app root per DEPLOYMENT.md §0.1 — `/home/rmis-mirdc/Desktop/RMISv4.1/.next/standalone/upload/` and `/home/rmis-mirdc/Desktop/RMISv4.1/upload/` if the folder still exists, (3) VM/host backups. Anything else was destroyed by earlier builds → re-upload.
- FIX (code): new `src/lib/upload-root.ts` — writer root resolution UPLOAD_DIR env → standalone auto-detect (`<deployRoot>/upload`, outside .next) → `<cwd>/upload` (dev, unchanged); readers probe LEGACY roots too (`resolveStoredFile`), deletes clear every candidate (`removeStoredFile`); traversal-guarded, framework-free. Wired into all 4 routes (documents POST root + profile-picture cleanup, [id] DELETE, extract (explicit "stored file is missing" failure), files serving with per-root containment).
- FIX (UI): document-preview.tsx now fetch-then-renders via blob URL (usePreviewAsset) and shows an explicit amber "File missing on the server — ask the applicant to re-upload" card (footer buttons hidden) instead of a blank PDF frame / broken image. Covers the review-queue dossier and applicant profile alike.
- FIX (ops): README runbook — `UPLOAD_DIR="$PWD/upload"` added to the PM2 block + new "Uploaded files (UPLOAD_DIR)" section: storage contract, one-time rescue `cp -rn` commands (standalone copy + old RMISv4.1 folder) to run BEFORE the next build, and the nightly `npm run backup` cron. DEPLOYMENT.md §0.2 correction note: UPLOAD_DIR was dead config until v4.3.
- FIX (backup): `scripts/backup.mjs` + `npm run backup` — copies db (+WAL/SHM) and the whole upload tree into backups/<timestamp>/, prunes to BACKUP_KEEP=14; DEPLOYMENT.md always documented this cron but the script never shipped. Smoke-tested (db + 52 files).
