Task ID: 83
Agent: Z.ai Code (main)
Task: Applicant QA 2026-10-22 — (1) add an "Other — type your own" option to the education form's Course / Degree field; (2) fix the MQR education evaluation so a custom-typed course does NOT block an applicant whose Education Level (e.g. College) already satisfies the position standard.

Work Log:
- ROOT CAUSE (explorer + engine trace): the unified engine's `eduLevelOf` inferred level by regex-scanning the CONCATENATED level+degree+course text with no abbreviation awareness and a level-1 fallback; the requirement tokenizer leaked a standalone "s" token from "Bachelor's" (apostrophe split) so passes were accidental; and CSC vocabulary standards tokenized into words no applicant row can contain ("Two years of college education (prior to 2018)" → ["two","education","prior","2018"]) — a latent landmine that failed EVERY applicant on such positions. Production still runs the pre-Task-78 engine, which is what the user's screenshot actually hit (educationLevel ignored entirely + exact-token overlap required).
- ENGINE DOCTRINE (src/lib/requirements.ts): (1) the explicit Education Level choice is now AUTHORITATIVE (level field mapped from the EDUCATION_LEVELS vocabulary first; degree/course/specifyOthers inference only as fallback for level-less rows, with BS/BSBA/AB/BA/MS/MA/MBA/PhD abbreviation expansion and a conservative no-signal default); (2) standards picked verbatim from CSC_EDUCATION_FIRST_LEVEL/HIGHER_LEVEL (normalized case/whitespace/curly-apostrophe) are LEVEL-ONLY clauses — minimum-level statements, not field-of-study requirements; (3) free-typed standards keep token overlap ("Bachelor's degree in Engineering" still requires an engineering course); (4) tokenizer drops single-letter artifacts and the degree-family abbreviations are stopwords so a bare "Bachelor's degree" reduces to a level check for ANY course. Ongoing-row completion law untouched; schoolName kept in the match text (permissive direction, reviewer sees evidence).
- CARD TAG (src/lib/credential-tag.ts): eduRank now delegates to the exported engine `eduLevelOf` — one doctrine, the kanban tag and the apply gate can never disagree.
- UI (Combobox "Other" escape hatch): new `otherOption` prop renders a persistent PenLine row at the foot of the panel (every state, last keyboard index, Enter-activated). Picking it: clears an exact listed choice, KEEPS already-typed custom text (just refocuses), keeps the panel open for typing; the typed text saves verbatim into Education.course and nothing is added to the reference vocabulary. Generic `allowOther` field-config flag in entry-form; only the education Course / Degree field opts in.
- VERIFICATION: 24/24 zero-DB unit checks against the real engine (reported scenario MET; HS/ongoing regressions hold; all 13 CSC vocabulary entries level-only with correct minima; free-typed field standards still discriminate; credential-tag parity). Live wire: minted applicant-5 session (zero DB writes) → POST /api/jobs/verify-mqr on the EXACT screenshot position (job 2, Cashier III, "Bachelor's degree") → Education "Meets the minimum requirements". Browser (agent-browser, minted sessions): Other row renders desktop + mobile; keep/clear/type behaviors proven in the real Edit Education dialog; education rows byte-unchanged afterwards (no saves); staff Create Job combobox re-verified (options + "Add …" row intact, no Other row). En route: discovered the Profile page's step nav was blocked by a "Quick tip" overlay and the MOM character-reference gate (expected behavior, applicant 3 has none — used applicant 5).
- INCIDENT-AVERSE NOTES: dev server :3000 healthy throughout; no DB writes anywhere (read-only sqlite checks + minted JWTs per the Task 82 lesson); scratch dir .tmp-t83 (JWTs + screenshots + unit script) deleted after verification.
- GATES: bun run lint CLEAN; bunx tsc --noEmit = exactly the 17 documented pre-existing errors in non-app files, zero in touched files. Committed dd7539a (6 files: requirements, credential-tag, combobox, entry-form, education-section, RELEASE-NOTES v4.6 section).

Task ID: 89
Agent: Z.ai Code (main)
Task: User clarification — job-education matching must be based on the Education LEVEL hierarchy (Graduate Studies > College > Vocational/Trade > Senior High School > Secondary > Elementary > Not specified), NOT the Course/Degree text; higher tier always meets a lower requirement.

Work Log:
- Verified the architecture first: ONE engine (lib/requirements.ts buildRequirementsReport) serves the apply gate (mqr.ts verifyMqr at POST /api/jobs/apply + /api/jobs/verify-mqr), the reviewer ledger, the review dossier and the kanban credential tag (eduLevelOf for relative sort only) — no drift possible.
- Confirmed the core rule was already correct for vocabulary standards: CSC_EDU_LEVEL_ONLY + the "relevant" rule strip course tokens, and the match is `applicant level >= required minLevel`. Production data: all 7 postings use Bachelor's-degree standards; the 7 Graduate Studies applicants already pass them.
- Found 2 real gaps vs the user's hierarchy: (1) "Not specified" as a requirement fell through detectLevel's default to COLLEGE tier — the exact opposite of the lowest tier; (2) Senior High School and Secondary shared rank 2, so a Secondary applicant passed a "Grade 12 / Senior High School graduate (starting 2016)" requirement.
- Fixed in requirements.ts: rank scale renumbered to the 7-tier hierarchy (Elementary 1 / Secondary+JHS+old-HS 2 / Senior High School-Grade12 3 / Vocational 4 / College 5 / Graduate Studies 6 / Doctorate 7); SHS tier inserted BEFORE the generic high-school tier in LEVEL_WORDS ("senior high school" contains "high school"); eduLevelOf + inferEduLevel renumbered with SHS tested before Secondary; partialCollege minLevel 4→5; isNotRequired now treats "not specified"/"not applicable" as NOT_REQUIRED; unparseable-free-text default stays College-tier semantically (4→5). Eligibility's separate scale untouched.
- Tested with a 19-scenario bun matrix covering the user's exact examples + regressions (all pass): Graduate Studies meets Bachelor's/College-tier standards regardless of course; Vocational meets SHS/JHS/old-HS standards; College does NOT meet Master's; Secondary no longer meets Grade-12/SHS but still meets old-HS and JHS; "Not specified" requirement is NOT_REQUIRED even for applicants with zero education rows; legacy uppercase 'COLLEGE' row passes; rank ordering assertion; verifyMqr end-to-end verdict.
- Production impact assessed: zero behavior change for the 7 live postings (all College-tier standards, and College/Graduate applicants rank identically relative to them); changes bite only on future SHS / vocational / not-specified standards. Lint clean, health 200. Commit c075e68.

Stage Summary:
- The engine now implements the user's clarified doctrine verbatim: Education Level rank is the sole basis for vocabulary/relevant standards, higher tier always qualifies for lower requirements, SHS strictly above Secondary, and "Not specified" requirements accept everyone.
- Display side stays consistent automatically (credential-tag + review ledger share the same engine).

- Updated deploy/mirdc_middleware/DEPLOY-NOTES.md (new section + ops recommendation); wrote deploy/RUNBOOK-email-preflight.md (5-minute go-live checklist: relay /health → /health?smtp=1 → RMIS /api/health → admin Send test email + Reply-To header check → real-address OTP; email_logs.error decoding table; recovery playbook: app-password revocation / 550 5.4.5 quota / security block / network; backlog-clearing guidance); DEPLOYMENT.md §0.4 gains the Task-92 pointer.
- Verified end-to-end in an isolated env (express+nodemailer+ejs installed fresh; capturing SMTP sink in pure asyncio; node --check clean first): /health exact old shape; ?smtp=1 "ready" with sink up; LEGACY sendMail → 200 original keys + messageId/accepted/rejected, From "Legacy System" <smtp user>, Reply-To = body.from, HTML verbatim; RMIS-shaped sendMail → Reply-To "MIRDC HR Office" <recruitment@mirdc.dost.gov.ph>, multipart/alternative text+html (Task 14 contract proven); html-object → EJS template renders; sink killed → 500 "Internal server error (SMTP: connect ECONNREFUSED 127.0.0.1:1025)" + probe "down (…)" + plain /health STILL green (process-up semantics); legacy wrong-token 400 {"error":"Invalid API value"} unchanged; missing-to 400 unchanged; blackholed SMTP (10.255.255.1:465) → probe capped at 4.02s "down (probe timed out after 4s)", sendMail answers 500 at 10.02s "Internal server error (SMTP: Connection timeout)" — inside the RMIS 15s abort. Lint clean. Commit 9d22dc1.
- RELEASE-NOTES v4.7 "Email relay production-readiness (Task 92)" section added.

Stage Summary:
- Parity with the old production repo: CONFIRMED identical (middleware, driver, env, docs) — the old repo's 2026-09-29 smoke test is on record.
- Oct-22 outage root cause class: VM-side hard SMTP failure hidden by a bare 500 + green /health; NOT volume, NOT RMIS.
- The relay drop-in now self-diagnoses: one curl (health?smtp=1) proves deliverability; failures carry the SMTP reason into email_logs.error; stalled sockets fail inside the caller's abort window.
- NEXT OPERATIONAL STEP (needs VM access, not sandbox): copy deploy/mirdc_middleware/index.js to the VM (backup → replace → restart → curl /health + /health?smtp=1 → one real test email per RUNBOOK-email-preflight.md §1.4).
